*{Accepted Mission: Long Day's Journey Into Night}*

### Head to the Parlor Car to join the navigation meeting

> *A new journey is about to begin. Pom-Pom has stated in the group chat that they want to stress "three things" during this navigation meeting. Given that the conductor rarely leaves people in suspense, perhaps you can mull over what they are thinking and play the role of the stern conductor's straight man for the navigation meeting. Find a suitable place in the Parlor Car to wait for everyone to arrive.*

*(Upon teleporting to the Parlor Car)*

**(Trailblazer):** (Time to head to the next stop. Let's meet up with everyone in the Parlor Car...)

> **开拓者：**（要出发前往下一站了，去观景车厢和大家会合吧……）

[Recap] As the Express headed toward Penacony, Pom-Pom reminded everyone to mind the memoria concentrations and The Family's rules. They also hoped that everyone can find traces of the three Nameless that were left behind.

*(Approach the Crew)*

**March 7th:** Pom-Pom's not here yet?

> **三月七：**列车长还没来吗？

**Himeko:** Pom-Pom knows how to keep people on the edge of their seats.

> **姬子：**帕姆也有卖关子的时候啊。

**Pom-Pom:** *ahem* Apologies for the delay, dear passengers. Something important came up, so I, the conductor, had to spend some time preparing!

> **帕姆：**咳咳，各位乘客久等了。因为是重要的事，所以列车长我花了点时间准备帕。

- ▸ So... what came up?

**March 7th:** Yeah! What was so important?

> **三月七：**对啊对啊，什么事这么重要？

- ▸ It's rare for the conductor to make an announcement.

**March 7th:** Yeah! What was so important?

> **三月七：**对啊对啊，什么事这么重要？

- ▸ Are we switching routes again?

**March 7th:** Huh? But I only just finished packing my bags!

> **三月七：**啊？我才刚刚收拾好行李欸！

**Pom-Pom:** I thought everyone knew! Our next destination is the Planet of Festivities, Penacony.

> **帕姆：**各位乘客应该都知道了帕？列车此行的目的地是「盛会之星」匹诺康尼。

**Pom-Pom:** I know everyone's eager to disembark and get to that famous stellar hotel, but before we set off, I should remind everyone of three things.

> **帕姆：**虽然本列车长也知道，大家对那座闻名宇宙的星空酒店期待已久，但在出发前，有三件事得再提醒下各位。

**Pom-Pom:** First: Penacony is located in Asdana, a star system brimming with memoria, which has historically been one of the Macro Voids that leaked out from the Memory Zone. Even after eons, the memoria concentration up ahead is above normal levels.

> **帕姆：**第一，匹诺康尼所处的阿斯德纳是一片「忆质」充盈的星系，历史上曾是忆域泄露的「大孔洞」之一。尽管过去了数千年，前方的忆质浓度仍高于均值。

**Pom-Pom:** Usually, this wouldn't be a huge problem... but everyone's different, so if you exhibit any signs of dizziness, hallucination, distorted memories or any other symptoms, you should take them seriously!

> **帕姆：**通常来讲是不会有什么大问题…但每个人的身体情况不同，一旦出现晕眩、幻觉或者记忆紊乱等问题，一定不要轻视！

**Pom-Pom:** Second: Penacony is a colony belonging to the Harmony Family — it also marks the first time this Family has extended a public invitation to other factions. Please remember that the Express has been invited as a guest, and that we must adhere to any and all regulations.

> **帕姆：**第二，匹诺康尼是「同谐」家族的属地，这也是家族首次向其他派系公开发出邀约。要记得，列车是以客人的身份受邀前往，该遵守的规矩一定要遵守。

**March 7th:** Got it! When in Rome, as they say! Don't worry, Conductor, we definitely won't embarrass the Nameless.

> **三月七：**懂的，就是入乡随俗呗！放心吧列车长，咱们绝不给无名客丢脸。

**Pom-Pom:** Rome? Anyway... Third and final: Rather than being a request... Pom-Pom has a favor to ask of everyone.

> **帕姆：**最后是第三点，与其说是要求…帕姆想向各位提出一个不情之请。

**Pom-Pom:** If you could, I hope that while you're all on vacation, you could spare some time to help inquire about a few of the Nameless.

> **帕姆：**如果可以——希望你们在度假之余，也能抽些时间，帮忙打听几位「无名客」的消息。

- ▸ What Nameless?
- ▸ Are we getting new crew mates?

**Himeko:** Allow me to explain.

> **姬子：**我来解释吧。

**Himeko:** Just like most of the worlds that the Express calls at, Penacony is one of the stops along the silver tracks. Several millennia ago, it was an IPC frontier prison, and it was the Trailblaze that connected it to thousands of stars.

> **姬子：**和列车停靠的大部分世界一样，匹诺康尼也曾是银轨上的一个站点。数千年前，这里还是公司的边陲监狱，是「开拓」将其与千星相连。

**Himeko:** During that time, the Astral Express once visited Penacony. As with all meetings, they inevitably lead to goodbyes. As the Express's logs indicate, it seems some passengers decided to call Penacony their permanent home.

> **姬子：**彼时，星穹列车也曾到访匹诺康尼——正如旅行有聚有散，根据列车记录，似乎有几位乘客选择了留在这里，将匹诺康尼选做自己的终点站。

- ▸ Is this a rare happening?
- ▸ Who could be this important?
- ▸ It's been so long. Will we still be able to find them?

**Himeko:** Don't worry, just think of it as returning to your hometown. Following the Express's departure, the Stellarons cut off contact to myriad worlds, and Penacony's ownership switched hands several times.

> **姬子：**别紧张，就把它当作一种重返故地的仪式吧。列车离开后，星核隔断诸界，匹诺康尼的归属也几度易主。

**Himeko:** Why did these Nameless choose to stay here? How did the course of their subsequent lives unfold, and what legacies did they leave behind? Retracing our predecessors' footsteps, that is in itself an adventure, wouldn't you agree?

> **姬子：**那些留在此地的老无名客们后来过得如何，经历了哪些事，又给这世界留下了怎样的痕迹…探寻先人们的足迹，也不失为一种冒险。

**Himeko:** Even if they left the stars behind them, their Trailblaze would not be over. That's also what the conductor thinks, isn't it?

> **姬子：**即便离开了星空，无名客的「开拓」也不会结束。列车长也是这么想的，对不对？

**Himeko:** According to the passenger list, the three people who got off were Tiernan, Legwork, and Razalina — the Express's guard, mechanic, and surveyor, respectively. No supplementary details are available.

> **姬子：**根据乘员名册，当年下车的三位乘客分别叫铁尔南、拉格沃克和拉扎莉娜，曾是列车的护卫、机修工和测绘师。除此之外就没有别的信息了。

**March 7th:** Just names and occupations? And they don't even sound like their real names either... This will be like looking for a needle in a haystack!

> **三月七：**只有名字和职业？听着还不一定是真名…这不是大海捞针吗？

**Himeko:** We'll just leave it to the stars of fate. Considering the Nameless' diversity... Perhaps we might even stumble upon their descendants, or, against the slimmest of chances, the very individuals themselves.

> **姬子：**随缘就好。考虑到无名客的多样性…我们说不定还能找到他们的后代，甚至有极小的概率——能见到本人哦。

**Himeko:** We'll conclude today's navigation meeting on this point. There's a bit of time left before we jump. In the meantime, might be a good idea to double-check your bags.

> **姬子：**那么本次航线会议就到这里吧。离跃迁还有一段时间，大家可以再花些时间检查行李。

**Pom-Pom:** I'll broadcast an announcement before we jump!

> **帕姆：**跃迁开始前，本列车长会广播通知的帕！

Before the warp begins, why not discuss your individual plans with everyone? Of course, you could also check in on the newest Interastral Peace Broadcast and see if the Crew can really make the headlines every day.*

### The Knocking at Ungodly Hours

The Knocking at Ungodly Hours

> *This warp jump is quite unusual. In the blink of an eye, you transition from the comfortable couch of the Parlor Car to an unfamiliar location. A stranger awakens you, claiming that both of you coincidentally share the same dream, and offers to guide you to an exit. Follow her guidance and leave this area.*

### Meet with March 7th

> *The woman in the dream seems to be trying to tell you something, though you don't know what it is. The next thing you know, she swings a blade at you, and the strange dreamscape disappears in an instant. When you open your eyes, you realize that the train has already arrived in Penacony. It looks like March 7th is waiting for you. Alight from the train with her.*

*(After completing sub-mission The Knocking at Ungodly Hours)*

## 050　The Knocking at Ungodly Hours　丑时三刻的敲门声

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：A Strange Dream　|　角色：Trailblazer; Acheron; Sunday; Robin; Firefly; Silver Wolf; Dr. Ratio; Aventurine; Sparkle; Black Swan; Misha

### Leave the strange room

> *This warp jump is quite unusual. In the blink of an eye, you transition from the comfortable couch of the Parlor Car to an unfamiliar location. A stranger awakens you, claiming that both of you coincidentally share the same dream, and offers to guide you to an exit. Follow her guidance and leave this area.*

[Recap] As the Express warped, you had a weird dream... In the dream, you met a strange woman. She guided you out of this Dreamscape between reality and the Memory Zone.

**???:** Not another one...

**???:** ...Come with me.

> **？？？：**…跟我来吧。

- ▸ Who are you?
- ▸ What is this place?
- ▸ Where are we going?

**???:** These questions... have no meaning. But, I will answer you as best as I can: This is the border between reality and the Memory Zone, a... dreamscape.

> **？？？：**这些问题…没有意义，但我会尽力为你说明：这里是现实与忆域的交界，一片…「梦境」。

**???:** Right this moment, you and I have coincidentally shared the same dreamscape, and appeared in each other's minds. This is the Land of Dreams's inaugural welcome for us.

> **？？？：**此时此刻，你我偶然分享了同一片梦境，出现在彼此的思绪中，这便是「梦想之地」对我们最初的问候。

**???:** Worry not, for very quickly, you will awaken from this dream, forget everything that has transpired, with naught but a faint lingering of loss.

> **？？？：**别担心，很快你就会从这场梦中醒来，忘记此间发生的一切，只留下淡淡的怅然。

**???:** Pay no mind, for this forgetfulness happens with each new dawn. It is an occurrence that we have become accustomed to for so long.

> **？？？：**也无需在意，这种「遗忘」发生在每一个清晨，是我们早已习惯的平常。

**???:** ...So, come with me. I'll take you home.

> **？？？：**…所以，跟我来吧。我会带你回家。

### Continue down the unfamiliar corridor

> *The warrior calls herself "Acheron," a Galaxy Ranger. Continue to follow in her footsteps and leave this strange dream.*

**???:** Left, to the end of the corridor. I will wait there for you.

> **？？？：**左边，走廊尽头。我在那里等你。

*(Head right, optional)*

**Acheron:** I'm not there. Turn back.

> **黄泉：**我不在那里，回头。

*(Approach Acheron)*

**Acheron:** Galaxy Ranger, Acheron... This is what they call me. You can call me whatever you want.

> *Acheron leads you to a door. Is this the dreamscape exit?*

*(Approach Acheron)*

**Acheron:** Open this door... Take a look at the mesmerizing dreamscape, while you can still remember.

> **黄泉：**打开这扇门…看看瑰丽的梦境吧，趁你还记得。

> *The warrior calls herself "Acheron," a Galaxy Ranger. Continue to follow in her footsteps and leave this strange dream.*

*(Enter through the door)*

**(Trailblazer):** (This is, no way...)

*(Approach Acheron)*

**Acheron:** This way.

> **黄泉：**这边。

**Acheron:** After you.

> **黄泉：**你先请。

### Speak with Acheron

> *After a disorienting exploration, you realize that you have somehow ended up on the lobby ceiling. Acheron seems to be long accustomed to this and simply points out the dreamscape exit just ahead.*

> **姬子：**想必是笔天文数字吧，所以现在，砂金先生，我这儿有一笔您不容错过的「投资」。

**Himeko:** This is the part that intrigues me — the invite sent by The Family was nothing more than a music box that, when wound, would play an accompanying tune.

> **姬子：**这就是耐人寻味的地方。家族送来的「邀请函」是一只八音盒，转动发条便会伴着谐乐发出声音。

**Himeko:** But the box that was sent to us was slightly unusual... The music it played had off-key notes in the background.

> **瓦尔特：**…还有件令我在意的事。当说到邀请函时，那位家族话事人是这么回应的：「作为匹诺康尼的实际管理人，家族理应为各位排忧解难」。

> **黄泉：**不过临走前，我可以再问你一个问题吗？在你听来，或许会有些古怪，甚至失礼，但我想知道……

You approach the Dreampool and hear the sweet voice of a female singer coming from all around the room, fragrance permeating the air. Together, these two elements envelop you in their embrace. The scent is sweet, profound, and luxurious — reminiscent of ripened fruits and a distant ocean painted by the hues of a setting sun.

Perhaps, it's time to enter the dream.

- ▸ Lie down in the Dreampool.

You curl your body, lying down in the center of the Dream Pool. The icy liquid rises above your waist. The gentle female voice continues to whisper in your ear, accompanied by the tolling of evening bells. The wild wind rustles through the barren plains, while nightingales, seagulls, swans, and ravens add their symphony to the choir...

Pendulum swings. You wonder what dream you'll delve into and slowly drift into drowsiness...

[Recap] Aventurine met with Dr. Ratio. The two came to Penacony as representatives of the IPC. Aventurine claimed that his monetary gifts and the aventurine stone bestowed upon him by the Amber Lord had been confiscated by Sunday, and that he needed to find more allies.

**Aventurine:** What is it, Ratio? Why the long face... Hey, I just realized, where's that handsome bust of yours?

> **砂金：**怎么了，拉帝奥？别愁眉苦脸的了…嘿，我才注意到，你那英俊的石膏头呢？

**Dr. Ratio:** You're late, by four minutes and sixteen seconds. You'd better have solved the mystery of Akivili's fall. Otherwise, I'd suggest not tangling with the Nameless.

> **真理医生：**你迟到了，整整4分16秒。你最好是用这段时间解开了阿基维利陨落之谜——如果没有，那我会忠告你别去找无名客的麻烦。

**Aventurine:** You mean you don't believe me when I say I truly wish to befriend them?

> **砂金：**找麻烦？连你也这么觉得？就没人相信我真的只是想和他们交朋友？

**Dr. Ratio:** Clamorous people do not make friends. Fun fact: The Attini Peacock boasts one of the universe's more jarring songs, and your choice of attire is somewhat peacock-esque.

> **真理医生：**聒噪的家伙可交不到朋友。一个小知识：阿蒂尼孔雀是宇宙中叫声数一数二难听的鸟类，而你这身行头正像一只花枝招展的孔雀……

**Dr. Ratio:** ...It seems this peacock is short of a feather or two. The Family's been rummaging through your things, haven't they?

> **真理医生：**…看来这只孔雀的羽毛还被人拔光了——行李被家族没收了？

**Aventurine:** Yeah, by that gray-suited fellow. All the cash gifts, and the box containing the Cornerstones... Eh? Where're you going?

> **砂金：**嗯，都被那穿灰西装的给扣了，所有的礼金，还有存放「基石」的匣子…嗯？你往哪走呢？

**Dr. Ratio:** Back home, to tell the corporation that some idiot ruined everything.

> **真理医生：**打道回府，告诉公司有个蠢货把一切都搞砸了。

**Aventurine:** It's nothing more than a few rocks, why so serious? Who cares if they're gone?

> **砂金：**几块石头罢了，这么悲观干嘛？没了又怎样？

**Dr. Ratio:** Without that aventurine stone, you're nothing more than a doomed Sigonian thrall sentenced to die by the IPC. Or is that serial number on your neck a cherished memento from the Amber Lord?

> **真理医生：**没了那块「砂金石」，你就只是个被公司判了死刑的茨冈尼亚奴隶——还是说，你脖子上那行「商品编码」也是琥珀王的恩赐？

**Aventurine:** ...Hah, you're funny, I'll give you that! It appears our erudite friend has done his homework.

> **砂金：**…哦，可以啊，幽默！看来我们这位博学的朋友是好好备过课了。

**Dr. Ratio:** It's my job. How would a gambler wrest Penacony back for the IPC otherwise?

> **真理医生：**这是我的工作，不然一个赌徒要怎么替公司收复匹诺康尼？

**Aventurine:** Don't worry, Doctor, I have my ways. The Family fears interference from the IPC, hence their constant intervention... Or, to play devil's advocate, perhaps Penacony might have always been a ruse under the guise of "the Harmony?"

> **砂金：**放心吧，教授，我有的是办法。家族害怕公司搞事，所以才处处针锋相对…搞不好，匹诺康尼从始至终就是「同谐」的阴谋。

**Aventurine:** If you ask me, that invite is nothing but a self-planted diversion, a red herring. Or maybe they're thinking of pulling off something big during the Charmony Festival...

> **砂金：**要我说，那封邀请函就是个自导自演的幌子，或许是他们想在谐乐大典上做点什么出格的事……

**Aventurine:** ...Or someone else made the invite, and they acquiesced to elevating the magnitude of the plan. Regardless — be it The Family or the Watchmaker's Legacy, as long as they're all useful to me.

> **砂金：**…又或者邀请者另有其人，但他们默许了，想将计就计再布个更大的。无所谓，家族也好，「钟表匠的遗产」也罢，能为我所用就行。

**Dr. Ratio:** Get to the point. What's your plan?

> **真理医生：**说重点。办法是什么？

**Aventurine:** No need to expand on that now. It's not yet time to reveal my trump card.

> **砂金：**现在没必要讲那么清楚——还不是亮出底牌的时候。

**Dr. Ratio:** Damned gambler. Any true collaboration demands trust. Did that notion somehow escape your Sigonian upbringing?

> **真理医生：**该死的赌徒，合作的前提是互相信任——茨冈尼亚人的学前教育里不包含这个吗？

**Aventurine:** Then, do you trust me?

> **砂金：**那你信任我吗？

**Dr. Ratio:** That depends on you.

> **真理医生：**这取决于你的态度。

**Aventurine:** So you don't trust me either. Now that was easy, wasn't it? Also, I didn't go to school, and my parents didn't teach me any of that. A pity, they left before they could impart any life lessons.

> **砂金：**所以你也不信任我，这不就行了？还有，我没读过书，我父母也确实没教过这个——很遗憾，他们还没来得及教就走了。

**Dr. Ratio:** ...I did not mean to offend.

> **真理医生：**…我无意冒犯。

**Aventurine:** I'm not offended. But they did teach me that friends are weapons of the Avgins. Even under the watchful eye of the Harmony, we need as many friends as we can get.

> **砂金：**别在意。但他们教过我「朋友就是埃维金人的武器」——在「同谐」虎视眈眈的情况下，咱们的「朋友」确实越多越好。

**Aventurine:** Let's see, the Garden of Recollection and the Astral Express have already made contact. The Annihilation Gang... I can't really count on them. The Knights of Beauty haven't given their answer, and as for that bunch at the Tavern...

> **砂金：**我想想啊，流光忆庭和星穹列车已经接触过了，泯灭帮…多半没戏，纯美骑士团还不知道是否会赴约，至于「酒馆」的家伙们……

**Aventurine:** ...Speaking of which, I ran into a woman just now who introduced herself as a Galaxy Ranger. The whole conversation felt off. Help me look into her...

> **砂金：**…说到这个，刚才遇见个女人，说是巡海游侠。我总觉得哪里不对劲，你去帮我探探她的底细……

**Aventurine:** ...Huh?

> **砂金：**…人呢？

**Aventurine:** ...You don't really disdain me that much, do you? Well, seems like I'm on my own again.

> **砂金：**…不至于这么讨厌我吧？唉，看来还是得靠自己了。

[Recap] After falling weightlessly, you encountered Robin and Sunday. Sunday claimed that Penacony is a world made on the foundations of memoria, Robin used the Harmony to restore your ability to move.

**Robin:** (Trailblazer)! Are you alright?

- ▸ The fall didn't hurt at all!

**Robin:** Haha, there's nothing to worry about — you've arrived at the Dreamscape.

**Sunday:** Seems like our guest here hasn't gotten used to the transition. Fret not, feeling a sense of weightlessness is normal for new visitors.

**Sunday:** Robin, please guide our friend in getting more comfortable with the new environment.

**Robin:** Leave it to me.

Robin gives a slight nod to her brother, then locks her emerald gaze with yours. She smiles, and you see ripples shimmering in the depths of her lake-green eyes, followed by a mighty vortex emerging from the depths of the pool.

Your vision dims as your body dissolves, becoming one with the fluidity of the time-space. You resonate with and become attuned to the serene fusion of frequencies. Your heart drums, leading this symphony.

**Robin:** Don't worry, just relax. You'll be feeling like your normal self in no time.

The choir starts. "Inhale." They sing. "Now Exhale." They chant. Instinct compels, and the stagnant fluid in your chest begins to flow again.

A gentle warmth emanates from within, saturating every inch of your being. Your melodies slow down, fade away, like a lullaby. A seven-colored illusion delicately caresses the top of your head and closes your eyes, its movements tender and compassionate. You feel contentment and drift into a deep slumber.

You open your eyes and feel a vitality like no other. The difficulty you were experiencing a moment earlier feels like a distant memory.

**Robin:** You should be feeling fully in control now. (Trailblazer), welcome to Penacony.

**Robin:** I've merely changed your state of mind for you to feel more at ease.

**Sunday:** Essentially, the dream of Penacony is a world formed by memoria. Only by fully understanding its nature can you move freely within it.

**Sunday:** It's like learning to swim — it's easy to sink at first when you aren't accustomed to the water, but once you get the hang of it you can float and swim on the surface effortlessly.

**Sunday:** Oops... I've fallen into my professional habits again. I just can't seem to shake off this urge to be a know-it-all.

### Follow Firefly and explore Penacony

> *The Bloodhound Family Security Officer explains that the young girl you just rescued was Firefly, an artist of the Iris Family. To thank you saving her, she has decided to be your tour guide for the day. Why not follow her and take a deep-dive into this beautiful dream?*

> **流萤：**这边这边！

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Firefly:** The first stop most people head to as soon as they arrive in the Dreamscape is the Oti Mall! The Dreamscape Sales Store here is super famous.

> **流萤：**谈起大多数人入梦后的第一站，那自然就是「奥帝购物中心」！这里的梦境贩售店超级出名。

> **流萤：**除此之外，还有各种奢侈品、服饰、潮流玩具店，甚至是车行——只要你的「苜蓿币」足够，想买什么东西都不成问题。

**Firefly:** SoulGlad is said to have originated from a sweet medicinal syrup popular during the Prison Era. Invented by a man named Sousa, it was thus called the Sousa Juice. Aideen, an interstellar traveling merchant, saw the potential in the beverage and purchased the formula.

> **流萤：**据说，苏乐达起源于监狱时期流行的甜味药水，由苏萨先生发明，因此得名苏萨水。后来星际行商艾迪恩发现其中商机，便买下了苏萨水的配方。

**Firefly:** Aideen then carbonated the beverage and renamed it "SoulGlad." He also pioneered the school of thought known as "Syrupism," in which he promoted the consumption of SoulGlad as a new kind of lifestyle. And just like that, he created a commercial miracle.

> **流萤：**你知道吗？苏萨水最初的配方中，有一种被称作梦见草的原料彻底灭绝了。所以现在只有在匹诺康尼的梦里才能尝到「元年苏乐达」的味道。

## 054　Good Omen, My Friend　好兆头，我的朋友

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Golden Hour　|　角色：Trailblazer; Firefly; Sampo; Dr. Edward; Hanu; Boss Stone　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Tales from the Golden Age'' completed

### Follow Sampo and experience "the adult side of things"

> *What surprises you most is that your old friend Sampo (okay, maybe friend is a strong word) happens to be in Penacony, too. Firefly doesn't seem to trust him very much, but the ever-friendly and enthusiastic dark-blue gentleman wants to teach you all about the "adult side" of things (in a legal way). Well, you're here now so, it couldn't hurt to try... Right?*

### Head to "Peppy Pepeshi's Salon"

> *What surprises you most is that your old friend Sampo happens to be in Penacony, too. Firefly doesn't seem to trust him very much, but the ever-friendly and enthusiastic dark-blue gentleman wants to teach you all about the "adult side" of things. According to him, your destination for this journey is a salon named "Peppy Pepeshi's Salon" — The name must have been intentional.*

*(Investigate pinball machine)*

### Lullaby of the North Wind

Lullaby of the North Wind

> *Sampo has led you back to the Dreamscape Sales Store and confessed to Dr. Edward the service reserved for regular customers (why didn't he say this earlier?), then ordered a customized dream bubble for you to experience. Though you have no idea what he is up to, your instincts fear that what you're about to experience is going to be... beyond your imagination.*

*(After pausing the challenge)*

### Search for Firefly

> *Sampo's previously relaxed and joyous attitude suddenly drops to a serious and stern tone as he warns you that there is something abnormal about Firefly's origin. After spouting a series of confusing but upsetting metaphors, he turns on his heel and leaves. At this moment, you realize that Firefly, who had been following you like a shadow, is suddenly nowhere to be seen. First things first, you must find Firefly, ensure her safety, and get an explanation.*

*(After completing sub-mission Lullaby of the North Wind)*

Bam! Green smoke wafts up from within the bag together with a loud noise. It twists and congeals in the air, slowly becoming a giant... trashcan.

**Garboski:** Finally — I am free! I am "Garboski," a can of unmatched evil. Tatalov used despicable means to defeat me and sealed me in this deep and cramped trash bag.

> **垃垃撕圾：**终于——我重获自由了！我是「垃垃撕圾」，一个无比邪恶的桶。塔塔洛夫用卑劣的手段击败我，把我封印进这深邃的垃圾袋。

**Garboski:** For the first five hundred years of my imprisonment, I swore an oath that if a can came to save me, I would reward them with endless wealth. Then, for the next five hundred years, I swore that if a can saved me, I would reward them with endless trash.

> **垃垃撕圾：**最开始的五百年，我发誓如果有桶来解救我，我必赐给他无穷的财富。第二个五百年，我发誓如果有桶来解救我，我必赐给他无尽的垃圾。

**Garboski:** However, ten thousand years have passed. That's 20 five-hundred-year periods, but no cans came! Therefore, I swore that if a can came to save me, I would reward them with a painless death.

> **垃垃撕圾：**但时间过去一万年——整整二十个五百年——始终没有桶来救我！于是我发誓如果有桶来解救我，我必赐给他无痛的死亡。

**Garboski:**— You've come, and so you now must die.

- ▸ Why not stand with me against Tatalov?

> **垃垃撕圾：**数万年的囚禁早就磨平了我的桶边，我已风光不再，只想以一个普通垃圾桶的身份度过余生。再见了，陌生的人。

**Garboski:** Even the vilest trashcan is capable of learning to heed a lesson. Your tactics are ineffective against me now! Now, feel my ten thousand years — five hundred years times twenty — of fury!

> **垃垃撕圾：**再邪恶的桶也懂得吸取教训，你这招已经无效了！现在，感受我被囚禁了一万年——整整二十个五百年——的愤怒吧！

[Recap] You saved the Trashcan Prince and obtained a Proof of Trust.

*(Talk to Trashcan Prince)*

This is a normal-looking trashcan. How magical! A kingdom of trashcans actually has an ordinary trashcan... This is a rare opportunity. Do you wish to do something to it?

- ▸ Insert trash.

Of course. That's the job of a trashcan. You chuck whatever random things you have on hand into its pitch-black and bottomless void. A series of loud sounds thundered back.

- ▸ Remove some trash.

Of course. That's the value of a trashcan. When you used your finger to touch its sides, a burst of loud sounds followed.

- ▸ Give it a kick.

...Evidently, this is not civilized behavior. In regular circumstances, you'd probably have to give up a Praise of High Morals... However, this is the Dreamscape, so who cares? You feel your foot impact the can with a loud bam...

When you open your eyes again, the trashcan is no longer just a trashcan. It's now a trashcan... with arms and legs.

**Trashcan Prince:** Thank you, beautiful stranger. I am the Trashcan Prince, and my father was assassinated by Tatalov, and I was cursed into becoming a trashcan. You saved me from internment.

**Trashcan Prince:** I shall offer everything I have — my body, my soul, my life, even my trash bags — just to repay your kindness.

- ▸ You talk like a Knight of Beauty...

**Trashcan Prince:** There are many cans who compliment my beauty, but I am not familiar with the name of which you just spoke. The only knightly order in this kingdom is the "Knights of Loose Change," each of whom is a noble and respectable can of the highest order.

- ▸ Then stand with me against Tatalov.

**Trashcan Prince:** I will. I will stand with you and ignite the rising smoke of rebellion until the kingdom is saved from absolute peril.

**Trashcan Prince:** You have earned my confidence. I wish for you to accept this item and my heart as a token of my sincere gratitude. I will fight for you, O glorious soul.

**Serious Can:** Then who is worthy of it?

**Beautiful Can:** Only I should have it!

[Recap] Three trashcans were arguing endlessly over who was the most "Pleasant-Looking Trash." You were compelled to deliver a just verdict to obtain a Proof of Trust.

*(Listen to three trash cans)*

Three trashcans stand around each other, arguing ceaselessly about a certain matter...

**Serious Can:** Outlander, a dispute awaits your fair judgment.

**Beautiful Can:** This is the legendary golden trash, a symbol of power and status. Only the most qualified trashcan may lay claim to it. All of us feel that we are that trashcan, and so we keep fighting for it.

**Wise Can:** So, we have decided that an observer possessing unique and just insight should be the judge for this matter and decide to whom the reward will belong, and bring a conclusive end to this long dispute.

**Serious Can:** If you give the golden trash to me, I will pledge you endless wealth, more than enough to fill the biggest trashcan there is.

**Wise Can:** If you give the golden trash to me, I will grant you endless knowledge, more than any full bag of trashcan provide.

**Beautiful Can:** If you bestow upon me this golden trash, I shall promise you the most beautiful trashcan in the world — myself. I will stand beside you as a partner beyond peer and face all your trials and triumphs by your side.

So... what is your choice?

- ▸ Split golden trash into three and distribute evenly.

Amidst the watchful gaze of the three, you perfectly split the golden trash. Not even a Clockie Pizza can be divided this evenly.

- ▸ I am the only one worthy of it!

Under the watchful eyes of the three, you claim the golden trash as your own. Indeed, among these small trashcans, is there anyone who stands as tall and upright as you do?

**Wise Can:** The conundrum that has stymied us for so long... fixed just like that?

**Beautiful Can:** Then who is the most qualified can... No, that no longer mattered.

**Serious Can:** The outlander must be trying to help us understand how foolish this dispute of ours was... You have proven yourself with your actions. This signifies our loyalty and longing. We wish for you to have it.

*(Obtain Proof of Trust (Mission Item))*

*(Talk to Wise Can again, optional)*

**Wise Can:** We all approve of your intellect and justness.

*(Talk to Serious Can again, optional)*

**Serious Can:** You have proven yourself with your actions. We three cans shall become your aides, and win freedom once more for trashcans.

*(Talk to Beautiful Can again, optional)*

**Beautiful Can:** We three cans will become your aides, and win freedom once more for our people.

*(Idle quote)*

**Trashiemedes:** Ninety-seven thousand eight hundred sixty-three...

[Recap] You encountered Trashiemedes, who was counting trash. If you wanted a Proof of Trust from them, you had to help them go through the last three cans.

*(Talk to Trashiemedes)*

This particular trashcan stands quietly. Its lid has chipped and faded paint. It is meticulously counting the roadside trash, occasionally sighing or scratching its head, as if struggling to memorize an astronomical number.

**Trashiemedes:** ...Are you here to mock me too? Or are you seeking wise answers?

- ▸ Why are you counting trash?

**Trashiemedes:** It's a long story. A long, long time ago, I angered Tatalov. I told him that even the most stable of nations has their day of decay, and even all the trash in the stars can eventually be counted. It is unclear which part of the sentence infuriated him...

**Trashiemedes:** ...As punishment, he assigned me this role, demanding that I count all the trash in the city. Before then, I am to never issue forth my opinions.

- ▸ I need "Proof of Trust."

**Trashiemedes:** If you want it, work with me. I have counted the majority of the trash in this city, leaving only these three newly arrived cans.

What sort of trash would Trashcan Kingdom hold? Curiosity piqued, you open the lid of the first trashcan.

Unfortunately, there isn't any difference. Maybe Dr. Edward did not have time to attend to these details.

[Recap] You discovered a comic in the second trashcan. In the comic, Tatalov once saved a human from the jaws of a giant scrap monster. To pay Tatalov back, the human transformed themselves into a trashcan.

There's a comic book inside this trashcan. The pages are torn and damaged. It tells of an adventure: Tatalov was once a brave trashcan. With valiance and willpower, it defeated a colossal junk monster and saved a human from its monstrous maw.

In order to repay Tatalov, that human turned himself into a trashcan... This is actually a horror story.

## 058　Should One Awaken at Midnight　倘若在午夜醒来

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：The Reverie (Reality)　|　角色：Trailblazer; Black Swan; Himeko; March 7th; Welt; Silver Wolf; Acheron; Firefly; Something Unto Death　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Through a Glass Darkly'' completed

[Recap] When you regained consciousness back in reality, you found out that it was Black Swan that brought you and Firefly out of the Memory Zone. Black Swan advised you to inform the Crew of your safety first, and that she will answer all your questions afterward.

**Black Swan:** You're awake, lazy bug. Looks like you had a nice dream.

> **黑天鹅：**你醒了啊，小瞌睡虫。看来你做了个好梦。

**Black Swan:** How was it? Did you dream of me?

**Black Swan:** I'm happy to see that you've kept your wits, and that you haven't been affected by the *Primal Dreamscape*. How lucky.

> **黑天鹅：**很高兴你的精神状态还算正常，没有受到那片*原始梦境*的影响，运气不错。

**Black Swan:** Heheh, you're quite concerned about that young lady. That's fair — she was hell-bent on assuring your safety even at the brink of death... Started to tug a little at my heartstrings, even.

> **黑天鹅：**呵呵，你很关心那个小姑娘啊。也是，即便在「死亡」面前，她也竭尽所能想要保护你的安全…连我都有些心动了呢。

**Black Swan:** I know you've many questions, so please compose yourself. Before that, you should let your friends know you're safe.

> **黑天鹅：**我知道你脑袋里有许多问号，别心急。在那之前，先向担心你的伙伴们报个平安吧。

[Recap] Black Swan claimed that the person who cast you into the Dreamscape was a Masked Fool named Sparkle, and the strange Memory Zone you saw came from Penacony itself. What you saw was the true form of the Dreamscape.

**Welt:** So, the one who attacked you was a girl dressed in red, adept at illusory arts, and was able to shapeshift...

> **瓦尔特：**所以，袭击你的人是一位身穿红衣，擅长幻术，能变化成他人样貌的女性……

**Black Swan:** Her name is Sparkle, a Masked Fool who has infiltrated Penacony... Don't worry. She won't be bothering you for now. She's probably patting herself on the back for a job well done.

> **黑天鹅：**她名叫「花火」，是混入匹诺康尼的假面愚者…放心，那姑娘暂时不会再打各位的主意了。她一定自以为得逞，正不知在何处得意洋洋呢。

**Welt:** It seems you know her well.

> **黑天鹅：**所以我有必要向各位澄清一个事实：花火小姐的把戏能令人陷入幻觉，但那片诡异的梦境和她无关，而是源自匹诺康尼本身。

**March 7th:** Penacony... itself?

> **三月七：**匹诺康尼…本身？

> **黑天鹅：**还没意识到吗？所谓的「梦想之地」…其实是家族不知用何种手段精心维护的成果，一场设计好的美梦。

**Black Swan:** And the Memory Zone he/she fell into, that's the true Dreamscape: Chaotic, dangerous, enigmatic... A beast of memory dwells in the ever-shifting labyrinth...

> **黑天鹅：**而他/她坠入的那片忆域——那才是梦境原本的样子，混乱、危险、神秘…变幻莫测的迷宫中栖息着记忆的野兽……

**Himeko:** ...She's right. Everyone, do you all still remember your surroundings during your entry into the Dreamscape?

> **三月七：**咦，原来每个人看到的是一样的么？我记得…然后是一间客房，再通过镜子来到一条长长的走廊……

> **瓦尔特：**「思绪长廊」——酒店的服务人员是这么称呼它的。

**Himeko:** Have you noticed? These landscapes seem quite distinct from that of Penacony's in your dream, yet strikingly similar to the Dreamscape described by Miss Black Swan.

> **姬子：**不觉得这些风景和梦中的匹诺康尼相去甚远…却和黑天鹅小姐描述的梦境十分相似么？

**Welt:** You're right... I didn't give it much thought at first, but in hindsight, the overlap is hard to ignore.

> **姬子：**以及，我从一些熟客口中得知，入梦后的第一站原本是「梦中的酒店」，但因为一些意外…「酒店目前正在进行修缮」。

**Black Swan:** What could possibly have to happen to an entire building for it to require renovation? Combine that with what he/she just experienced, and the answer is clear.

> **黑天鹅：**请问一栋建筑在什么情况下才需要修缮？结合下他/她方才的经历，答案很明了了。

[Recap] Black Swan claimed that the Dreamscape was breaking down and that Penacony was not completely safe. This was something that The Family was keeping a secret. Thus, she proposed a cooperative effort between her and the Express in exchange for the memories of the Trailblaze.

**Black Swan:** Penacony's dream is *sinking* right now, turning back to its original form... and falling into the deep sea.

> **黑天鹅：**匹诺康尼的美梦正在*沉没*，变回它原本的样子…坠入深海。

**March 7th:** Sinking? You mean... the Dreamscape is collapsing?

> **三月七：**沉没？你的意思是…梦境世界正在瓦解？

**Black Swan:** The encounter at the dreamscape Hotel was a sign.

**Welt:** ...I see. That would explain the uneasiness we've sensed from The Family.

> **瓦尔特：**倘若这一消息被外界得知，无论住客们的实际安全如何，盛会之星的名誉必然会受到影响。谐乐大典将至，他们不得不隐瞒此事。

**Black Swan:** Even the guests' safety cannot be assured now... Isn't the monster that he/she encountered indisputable proof? Need I remind you that it is a Memory Zone meme...

> **黑天鹅：**住客们的安全，也许这一点也无法保障哦…他/她遭遇的那只怪物不就是最好的证明吗？要我再提示一遍吗，它是忆域迷因……

> **黑天鹅：**暗喻死亡和谋杀的迷因怎会出现在家族宣称「绝对安全」的梦里…在匹诺康尼，所有人都可能说谎——或许也包括家族自己哦？

**Himeko:** Miss Black Swan, we fully understand our current predicament now. Going back to your original suggestion...

**Black Swan:** Hmm, let me rephrase then... this is my personal request, not the Garden of Recollection's. And the answer is simple. Miss Himeko, I simply wish to make an exchange... related to the Remembrance.

> **黑天鹅：**嗯，请允许我纠正一下…这是我个人的请求，不代表忆庭。答案很简单，姬子小姐，我只是想做个交换…有关「记忆」的交换。

**Black Swan:** Apart from my duty, I also have some... personal pursuit in aesthetics. I am a Memokeeper, and a collector. I, too yearn to experience those luminous, dazzling, cherished memories. That's simple enough to understand, no?

> **黑天鹅：**在职责之外，我也有些…个人的美学追求。我是忆者，也是一位收藏家，我也想见证那些晶莹、璀璨的宝贵记忆——这种想法很好理解，不是吗？

**Black Swan:** As for you all, Nameless of the Astral Express, and practitioners of the Trailblaze... I have faith in your potential, as well as in the... unique splendor you will unleash on the stage.

> **黑天鹅：**而你们，星穹列车的无名客，「开拓」之道的践行者…我相信你们的潜质，也相信你们将在这片舞台上绽放出的…独一无二的光辉。

**Black Swan:** This is why I wish to befriend all of you. A place where nightmares and sweet dreams intersect... the perfect cradle for the Remembrance.

> **黑天鹅：**这就是我想和各位交朋友的原因。噩梦与美梦的交汇之地…作为「记忆」的摇篮再合适不过了。

**Himeko:** ...I understand. But before replying, we must first discuss this among ourselves.

> **姬子：**…我明白了。但在答复你之前，我们还需要做些讨论。

### Speak with the Express Crew and discuss Black Swan's suggestion

Everyone says that the Memokeepers of the Garden of Recollection are the most fearsome... You should discuss your decision carefully before giving her an answer.*

[Recap] Himeko believed that the Dreamscape's changes had something to do with the "Watchmaker's Invitation." Welt said that someone at Penacony claims to have witnessed a tall figure in silver armor, and that they wished to share a message with you.

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**Welt:** ...How do you see things, Himeko?

> **姬子：**但我会担心最坏的可能，假如梦境的异变确实存在，并且不是自然发生，而是有人在幕后推动……

> **瓦尔特：**暂且不考虑极端情况，推动梦境异变的主使，立场定然与家族对立，那就不外乎两种可能……

> **瓦尔特：**有人意图引入外部势力，借机动摇家族对匹诺康尼的掌控；或是家族为了自保，被迫暗中向外界求援。

**March 7th:** Well... this doesn't necessarily mean a thing, right? In terms of methods, both the Fools and the Riddlers could pull it off. Hasn't the IPC cracked the message, too?

> **三月七：**但…这也不说明什么吧。只谈手法，愚者和谜语人也做得到，公司不也破译成功了吗？

**Himeko:** March, calm down. It's just a possibility. But if this encrypted message isn't some harmless prank, but done so to invite the Trailblaze into the fray, we cannot remain uninvolved.

> **姬子：**小三月，别紧张，这只是一种可能性。可如果这行密文不是单纯的恶作剧，而是什么人有意邀请「开拓」入场，我们就更没有理由坐视不管。

**Welt:** Well, let me tell you what I discovered, and I'm sorry — it's bad news.

> **瓦尔特：**那来说说我的发现吧——很遗憾，是个坏消息。

> **瓦尔特：**据可靠消息称，一些人在匹诺康尼目击到了身穿银色盔甲的高大男性。我向猎犬家系打听了消息，也走访了不少声称见过这位入侵者的来宾……

**Welt:** ...And this is what I received. (Trailblazer), I've sent it to you. Open it and take a look.

> **瓦尔特：**…然后收到了这个。开拓者，我发给你了，打开看看吧。

[Recap] Silver Wolf sent a message to the Express Crew, claiming that the so-called Stellaron Hunter Sam had already arrived at Penacony. Silver Wolf transmitted a set of codes to the Crew which allowed you to access the Deep Dreamscape you experienced before.

**???:** Ahem, can you hear me? Astral Express, it's been a while. How's Penacony?

> **？？？：**咳咳，能听到吗？星穹列车，好久不见——匹诺康尼好玩吗？

**March 7th:** It's... It's that hacker girl!

> **三月七：**这是…是那个骇客小姑娘！

**???:** I'll get straight to the point. I know you've all been investigating the abnormal phenomenon surrounding Penacony. Or rather, we are pleased to see that you've been doing that — because the deeper you delve, the closer you'll get to uncovering truth about this so-called "beautiful" dream.

> **？？？：**说正经的。我知道各位正在四处调查匹诺康尼的异象，不如说，我们非常乐于看见这一幕。因为你们调查得越深入，就越有机会了解到美梦的真相。

**???:** The Family is obviously hiding something, right? Well we've nabbed some valuable information, and out of the goodness of my heart I've decided to share it with all of you. Although, it unfortunately means your idyllic break in paradise is nearing its end.

> **？？？：**家族正在隐瞒什么，不是吗？我们也掌握了一手情报。现在，我决定宽宏大量地把它与各位分享。只是，可惜——美好的假期要结束啦。

**???:** Enter this string of code into your Dreampool — the device you use to dream with — and dive into the hidden map for a look! The coordinates have been sent to you. You just have to copy them over.

> **？？？：**把这段代码输进你们的入梦池，就是那个用来做梦的装置，到隐藏地图去看看吧！位置坐标都给你们打包好了，喏，一键复制就行。

**???:** Also, I take it you've already heard about Sam. You should look forward to the meeting. He's a straight shooter who values a fair fight. I think you'll *get along* with him just fine. Don't be overwhelmed by his enthusiasm!

> **？？？：**以及——想必你们已经听说萨姆的事了。好好期待一下吧，那家伙性格单纯，喜欢堂堂正正的胜负，一定与你们*合得来*。希望各位别被他的热情压垮！

**???:** Oh, he had me relay a message to you: The curtains have just risen over the dream that can't be reached. The various factions have started to move. I wish you luck! Don't fall behind, Nameless!

> **？？？：**哦，他让我转告各位：无法抵达的梦中，剧目即将开演——加油吧，各方势力都动起来了。无名客，别落后太远哦！

[Recap] Welt claimed that the Molten Knight Sam was once a genetically altered member of Glamoth's Iron Cavalry and was an extreme threat. After some discussion, you decided to explore the Dreamscape and seek out the truth before informing The Family.

**March 7th:** Those Stellaron Hunters... They even factored Penacony into their plan! Nowhere's safe from them!

> **三月七：**这群星核猎手…竟然在匹诺康尼也有布局，真是纠缠不休！

**Welt:** The situation is clear — the Planet of Festivities isn't what it seems on the surface. Everyone's sights, including the factions in The Family, are set on the Watchmaker's Legacy. Every party has its own motives, and they're attempting to thwart each other.

> **瓦尔特：**形势很明朗了。盛会之星远没有表面上看起来那么简单，围绕着「钟表匠的遗产」，包含家族在内的一众势力各怀目的，彼此制衡。

**Welt:** While we still don't know what this legacy entails, it's only a matter of time before the power struggle ensnares innocent bystanders.

> **瓦尔特：**尽管还不知道这遗产是什么，但这场争夺战波及无辜群众…是早晚的事。

**Welt:** The Molten Knight, Sam, is reportedly a remnant of Glamoth's Iron Cavalry — a genetically engineered warrior. He possesses superhuman perception and acts decisively — he'll leave no margin for error. This individual is every bit as dangerous as Kafka and Blade.

> **瓦尔特：**「熔火骑士」萨姆，据称此人是格拉默铁骑的余党，先天的基因改造战士，认知异于常人，行事决绝、不留余地，是不亚于卡芙卡和刃的危险分子。

**Welt:** This message is a threat. The hidden map that Silver Wolf mentioned is probably the sealed dreamscape Hotel. For the sake of what's to come, a visit seems necessary.

> **瓦尔特：**这是一封威胁信。银狼口中的隐藏地图想必就是封锁中的梦境酒店。为后续考虑，我们确实有必要拜访一下现场。

- ▸ Should we inform The Family?

> ▸ 要通知家族吗？

**Welt:** We may be on amicable terms, but that doesn't mean they trust us... Let's act first and talk later.

> **瓦尔特：**虽然态度友好，但他们未必信任我们…先行后闻吧。

- ▸ What if we get found out...

> ▸ 被发现了怎么办…

- ▸ It could be a Stellaron Hunter's trap...

> ▸ 也许是星核猎手的陷阱…

**Welt:** Which is why I didn't suggest it initially. But the way things are now, the Astral Express has a reason to join the fray.

> **瓦尔特：**所以我没有第一时间提出，但现在看来，星穹列车有入场的理由。

**Welt:** If The Family asks, just tell them that the Express is investigating the Stellaron Hunters — I'm sure they'll understand. We'll plan our next move according to how they react.

> **瓦尔特：**如果家族问起，就如实告知列车组在追查星核猎手，相信能得到理解。根据对方的反应，我们再采取下一步行动。

**Himeko:** Then we are in agreement. Let's return and inform Miss Black Swan.

> **姬子：**看来我们达成共识了——那就回去通知黑天鹅小姐吧。

### Speak with Black Swan and give her an answer

> *After considering every factor, you have decided to cooperate with this Memokeeper. Now, it is time to give her the conclusion that you have reached.*

[Recap] Himeko told Black Swan that the Crew was willing to cooperate, but just to be safe, Welt will remain in reality to prepare for negotiations with The Family.

*(Talk to Black Swan)*

**Himeko:** Miss Black Swan, the Express is willing to cooperate with you — we need a proper guide to navigate the Memory Zone.

> **姬子：**黑天鹅小姐，列车组愿意与你合作。在忆域中，我们需要一位合适的向导。

**Black Swan:** I'm at your service. I will not disappoint.

> **黑天鹅：**听凭差遣，不会让各位失望的。

**Himeko:** (Trailblazer) and March, return to your own rooms. Enter the dream, and if everything goes without a hitch... we'll see one another at the dreamscape Hotel's lobby.

> **姬子：**开拓者和小三月，回到各自的房间吧。准备入梦，如果顺利…我们在梦境酒店的大堂集合。

**Welt:** I'll stay in the real world to ensure everyone's safety. If needed, I will also parlay with The Family. I assume there's no problem, Miss Black Swan?

> **瓦尔特：**我会留在现实，确保各位安全无虞。如有需要，也会出面与家族交涉——没问题吧，黑天鹅小姐？

**Black Swan:** It seems that I'll have a hard time gaining your trust even if I personally did rescue your friends... It's no problem, of course, other than the fact that my feelings are a little hurt.

> **黑天鹅：**看来即便我亲手拯救了你们的同伴，各位也还是很难信任我呢…当然没问题，只是有点伤害我的感情。

**Himeko:** We're counting on you, Welt. As for us... Let's get ready to see the Dreamscape's true visage.

> **姬子：**拜托你了，瓦尔特。至于我们…就准备好一睹「梦境的真容」吧。

### Enter the Dreampool

> *Although the negotiation process was a little uncertain at times, at least everyone is happy with the results. With Black Swan's company, you should be able to avoid any danger in the "real Memory Zone." Enter the code that Silver Wolf gave you into the Dreampool and prepare to witness the true nature of the Dreamscape.*

**(Trailblazer):** (We'll be busy for a while. I wonder if Firefly's returned to reality yet...)

> **开拓者：**（看来要忙一阵子了，不知流萤回现实了没有……）

**(Trailblazer):** (...Hmm, I don't have her contact info, I'll ask Black Swan...)

> **开拓者：**（…不对，我没她的联系方式啊。问问黑天鹅吧……）

[Recap] You remained in the hotel room and chatted with Black Swan, sharing your thoughts with her. As a Memokeeper, Black Swan does not need to enter the Dreampool, and can accompany you into the Dreamscape from where she stands.

*(Talk to Black Swan, optional)*

**Black Swan:** You look anxious, (Trailblazer). Worried about that girl?

> **黑天鹅：**你看起来很紧张啊，开拓者。是在担心那个小姑娘吗？

**Black Swan:** You can talk to me if you've something on your mind. In this world, there's no one who heals the heart better than a Memokeeper.

> **黑天鹅：**如果有心事，不妨向我倾诉吧。这世上可没有比忆者更适合做心理治疗师的存在了。

- ▸ Thank you for saving me.

> ▸ 感谢你的救命之恩。

**Black Swan:** I just did what I had to do. You are special, after all... So special that many pairs of eyes are watching your every move.

> **黑天鹅：**别客气，这是我应该做的。毕竟你很特殊…特殊到有许多双眼睛在注视着你。

- ▸ Can you confirm Firefly is safe?

> ▸ 你能确认流萤的安危吗？

**Black Swan:** Don't worry. That is a one-way door leading to awakened reality. Unless that girl is so stubborn that she refuses to wake up from the dream — which isn't possible — she must be holed up somewhere in the hotel massaging her eyes right now.

> **黑天鹅：**放心吧，那是一道单向门，只会通往清醒的现实。除非那女孩抱有强烈的意愿，怎么也不肯从梦中醒来…怎么可能呢？她现在一定正在酒店的某处揉眼睛呢。

- ▸ To be honest, I don't really trust Memokeepers.

> ▸ 说实话，我不太信任忆者。

**Black Swan:** It appears you've had some unhappy memories... But it's obvious that this partnership makes the most sense to you, me, and the Express Crew, no? So you can take it easy and trust me.

> **黑天鹅：**看来你有些不太愉快的回忆呢…但显然，接下来的合作对你、对我、对列车组都是明智的，对吧？所以你可以安心地依赖我哦。

**Black Swan:** Of course, I'm not the only Memokeeper who has come — Penacony has opened its doors to factions beyond The Family for the first time, and the Garden must record this historic moment...

> **黑天鹅：**当然，前来这里的忆者不止我一人——匹诺康尼首次向家族以外的派系公开发出邀约，忆庭要铭记下这历史性的一刻……

**Black Swan:** So you can try working together with others as well... As long as you can see them, hehe.

> **黑天鹅：**所以你也可以试着和别人合作哦…只要你看得见他们，呵呵。

- ▸ You're not returning to your room?

> ▸ 你不回房间吗？

**Black Swan:** No. I will accompany you into the dream to prevent harm from befalling you all. Memokeepers don't need a Dreampool anyway.

> **黑天鹅：**不呀。我要陪你们一起入梦，免得你们受伤。反正忆者也不需要什么入梦池…对吧？

- ▸ That's all for now.

> ▸ 就聊到这里吧。

**Black Swan:** All right. Drift peacefully off to sleep, and I'll be right with you.

> **黑天鹅：**好呀~那就安心去睡吧，我会好好陪着你的。

*(Enter Silver Wolf's code)*

Welcome to Penacony, honored guest...

- ▸ Lie down in the Dreampool.

> ▸ 在入梦池中躺下。

- ▸ Not yet.

> ▸ 再等等。

Either way, you must meet with her.*

[Recap] Under Black Swan's guidance, you once again returned to the mysterious and eerie Dreamscape.

**Black Swan:** I shall use my methods to relieve you. Don't worry, as your companions are safe — I had both of them each give me a trinket before I entered the dream. This will allow me to feel their presence in the Memory Zone.

> **黑天鹅：**我会略施手段让你不那么难受。别担心，你的伙伴也很安全，我在入梦前向她们两人各要了一件小首饰，这能让我在忆域中感应她们的存在。

**Black Swan:** They're already awake in their rooms. Will you be comfortable in following me now? Time waits for no one. We must hurry to the lobby.

> **黑天鹅：**她们已在各自的房间里醒来了。这下，你应该就能放宽心跟我来了吧？时间不等人，这就出发去大堂吧。

### Enter the suspicious elevator

> *You are just a step away from the place you arranged to meet, but a suspicious elevator blocks your path. Black Swan says that the road ahead "seems to have been heavily distorted" — But what could possibly have happened in there? It seems you may have no choice but to enter the elevator to find your answer.*

### Interrogate Black Swan

> *As Acheron wages a ferocious battle against the Stellaron Hunter, Black Swan abducts you and takes you before Aventurine. Aventurine expresses his request to you, but you still cannot shake off your shock and the (to an extent) rage at being betrayed. Perhaps you should ask the Memokeeper just what she is planning.*

[Recap] You declined Aventurine's invitation and returned to reality to regroup with your companions. Under the Express Crew and The Family's shared effort, the IPC's plan to reclaim the planet failed. The Charmony Festival proceeded as intended, until all eternity.

*(Approach door on the first floor, optional)*

Aventurine isn't here. You know that, and so does he. You can also choose to turn around and find out what the facts are directly from his mouth, but if you insist on going forward, that signals a "rejection."

- ▸ Leave directly.

> ▸ 直接离开。

After careful consideration, you decline Aventurine's invitation. This time, he simply watches as you depart from the Memory Zone without asking you to stay.

Returning to reality, you reunite with your companions. After a thorough discussion, the Crew decides to assist Penacony, leading to the revelation of a conspiracy orchestrated by the IPC.

With the expulsion of all ill-intentioned visitors, the Charmony Festival proceeds smoothly. As honored guests, you partake in the joyous atmosphere, filled with laughter, clinking glasses full of SoulGlad, and the exuberant singing and dancing of the crowd...

"May this moment last forever, and may we never awaken from this euphoric dream!"

*(Talk to Black Swan)*

**Black Swan:** My, my... you look like a small injured animal. Are you still willing to talk to me? Of course... I'm still willing to be your psychotherapist.

> **黑天鹅：**呀…表情像是受伤的小动物呢。你还肯和我谈谈吗？我依旧愿意…当你的心理治疗师。

- ▸ You betrayed me?

> ▸ 你欺骗了我？

**Black Swan:** I admit I'd made a transaction with that gentleman to bring you before him. But... this is also a safe place.

> **黑天鹅：**我承认和那位先生做了交易，把你带到他的身边。但…这里同样是安全的地方。

**Black Swan:** He needs you. It's essential if we want to reclaim Penacony. Recognizing his immense potential and the invaluable memories he can help me gather, I opted to join forces with him.

> **黑天鹅：**他需要你，这是收复匹诺康尼必需的一环，而我认为他有潜力，也能助我收获独一无二的记忆，便答应了合作。

**Black Swan:** In Penacony, everyone may be lying, and yes... that includes me. I can't deny this, nor do I seek forgiveness.

> **黑天鹅：**在匹诺康尼，所有人都可能说谎，当然…也包括我，这点我无可辩驳，也不会奢求原谅。

**Black Swan:** But do you still remember? I said... "I have faith in your potential," and that's not a lie.

> **黑天鹅：**但，还记得吗？我也说过…「我相信你们的潜质」，这句话绝无半点虚假。

**Black Swan:** The Garden of Recollection wants to witness the future of the Astral Express Trailblazing the cosmos, and as for these memories — I will treasure them. I do not have reasons to hurt any of you. Instead, I will continue to assist you — provided you are willing to accept my assistance.

> **黑天鹅：**忆庭想见证星穹列车开拓银河的未来，而我会将这些回忆一一珍藏。所以我没有伤害各位的理由，反而会继续帮助你们——前提是你们还愿意。

- ▸ Can I trust Aventurine?

> ▸ 砂金值得信任吗？

**Black Swan:** My take on this might surprise you.

> **黑天鹅：**我的看法或许会出乎你的意料。

**Black Swan:** In my view, he's a trustworthy individual. Not for any other reason, but because he's an exceptional businessman. In this vast universe, no one values honesty and contracts more than a merchant.

> **黑天鹅：**在我看来，他是个值得信任的人，不为别的，只因他是个优秀的商人。在这茫茫寰宇中，没有人比商人更注重诚信和契约。

**Black Swan:** But, always be on guard, particularly when making agreements with him. Scrutinize the details. Beyond the confines of the contract, your rights are off his radar... He'll go to extreme lengths to ensure his advantage.

> **黑天鹅：**但你也要当心，尤其在与他们订立契约时，要多加留意。在契约之外，他们不会考虑你的权利…为了最大化自己的利益，他们可以无所不用其极。

- ▸ Who do you think is the mastermind...?

> ▸ 你觉得幕后黑手是谁…

**Black Swan:** I still believe that Miss Firefly's situation... is deeply connected with the Stellaron Hunter, just as we speculated. But as you've heard, Aventurine has his finger pointed at the Galaxy Ranger.

> **黑天鹅：**我依旧认为流萤小姐的事…如我们推测的那般，和星核猎手关联更甚。但你也听到了，砂金指控了那位游侠。

**Black Swan:** I can't fathom why he'd draw that conclusion, but given his access to the IPC's intelligence network, we should seriously consider his perspective. Moreover, I believe Miss Acheron is also hiding something.

> **黑天鹅：**虽然不知他为何会作出这种判断，但他有公司的情报网，看法值得深入讨论。以及，我也认为黄泉小姐有所隐瞒……

**Black Swan:** ...No, in fact, I should say, she's hiding everything from us.

> **黑天鹅：**…不，应该说，隐瞒了全部的事。

**Black Swan:** It was an unexpected finding from an earlier encounter. With regards to the Annihilation Gang... I can also prove that what Aventurine said was the truth.

> **黑天鹅：**这是在此前的一次邂逅中，我意外得到的收获。关于泯灭帮…我也能证明砂金所说确有其事。

**Black Swan:** That's why, during this golden soirée... she is the dance's centerpiece.

> **黑天鹅：**也因此，在这场金色的宴会中…她是我最关注的舞者。

- ▸ Are you willing to bring me out of this Memory Zone?

> ▸ 你愿意帮我走出这片忆域吗？

**Black Swan:** Why of course, but now's not the time.

> **黑天鹅：**我非常愿意，但现在还不是时候。

**Black Swan:** I know you're confused and... sad. Whoever that girl might have been, a living soul, a "memory" that was meant to last... she simply vanished, like the dissolution of bubbles in water, disappearing in an instant...

> **黑天鹅：**我知道你很疑惑，也很…悲伤。无论那女孩是谁，一个活生生的人，一段不应结束的「记忆」…就这样被抹去了，仿佛溶解在水中的泡沫，烟消云散……

**Black Swan:** No matter what, I hope you can trust Aventurine — or rather — trust me and see the "truth" with your own eyes. This can lead us deeper into the secrets behind Penacony.

> **黑天鹅：**无论如何，我希望你信任砂金——或者更多地——信任我，去看看他口中的「事实」。这能带领我们走向匹诺康尼背后更深的秘密。

**Black Swan:** I will protect you. Once I gather more information from him, I'll make sure you're safely returned to your companions.

> **黑天鹅：**我会保护你。等从他手中获取了更多信息，我再护送你回到同伴身边也不迟。

**Black Swan:** For the truth and to prevent more unnecessary sacrifices... It is the right choice.

> **黑天鹅：**而为了真相，以及制止更多无谓的牺牲…这是最正确的选择。

- ▸ You're right. Let's go.

> ▸ 你说得对，我们走吧。

- ▸ How would I know you're not trying to frame me?

> ▸ 我怎么知道你们不是想陷害我？

**Black Swan:** Perhaps he plans on doing so, but I won't.

> **黑天鹅：**他或许有这个打算，但我不会。

**Black Swan:** The Garden of Recollection has eyes everywhere. If he wants to hurt you, he'll have to fight against the Memokeepers — I've warned him about that.

> **黑天鹅：**忆庭的视线无处不在。如果他想对你动手，就得做好与忆者为敌的准备——这点我已经提醒过他了。

**Black Swan:** Later, I'll stay by your side as a memetic entity, just in case. Time to set off, (Trailblazer).

> **黑天鹅：**接下来，我会以模因形式陪伴在你身边，以备不时之需。出发吧，开拓者。

### Find Aventurine

> *The Memokeeper similarly expresses mistrust of Aventurine, but convinces you that it is for the greater good. You decide to go and see what this truth that he speaks of is — the truth that could "overturn Penacony."*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**(Trailblazer):** (Go and meet Aventurine, or... I could still choose not to help...)

> **开拓者：**（去见砂金吧，或者…我还是可以选择拒绝……）

*(Approach door on the first floor, optional)*

**Black Swan:** Are you still... continuing down this path? This is a rare opportunity. Leaving here... will also mean leaving the truth.

> **黑天鹅：**这个方向…你还是要走吗？这可是千载难逢的机会，离开这里…也就意味着离开真相。

- ▸ You're right. I can't leave.

> ▸ 我已决定，离开这里。

**Black Swan:** Yes, I better think about Aventurine's proposal.

> **黑天鹅：**这样啊…嗯，我尊重你的选择。我们走吧，回现实去。

- ▸ I've decided. I'm leaving this place.

> ▸ 你说得对，不能离开。

**Black Swan:** I see... Fine, I respect your choice. Let's return to reality.

> **黑天鹅：**嗯，还是考虑一下砂金的提议吧。

After careful consideration, you decline Aventurine's invitation. This time, he simply watches as you depart from the Memory Zone without asking you to stay.

Returning to reality, you reunite with your companions. After a thorough discussion, the Crew decides to assist Penacony, leading to the revelation of a conspiracy orchestrated by the IPC.

With the expulsion of all ill-intentioned visitors, the Charmony Festival proceeds smoothly. As honored guests, you partake in the joyous atmosphere, filled with laughter, clinking glasses full of SoulGlad, and the exuberant singing and dancing of the crowd...

"May this moment last forever, and may we never awaken from this euphoric dream!"

[Recap] Aventurine was pleased with your decision to cooperate with him. He invited you to turn the tables and exploit him instead, to prove the value of your collaboration. Led by Aventurine, you headed to where the truth was.

*(Talk to Aventurine)*

**Aventurine:** Excellent, I knew you would come. By the way, about that Memokeeper... Never mind, I won't press the issue further.

> **砂金：**很好，我就知道你一定会来。对了，那位忆者…算了，我不多问了。

**Aventurine:** I've said before, you can liaise with your companions, or even twist the narrative against me. I'll wholeheartedly embrace it! It signifies your potential.

> **砂金：**毕竟我说过，你可以和同伴商量，也可以反过来利用我，我很欢迎！因为这也是在向我展示你们的价值。

**Aventurine:** I don't do deals where I'm on the losing end. So, my "friends"... don't let me down.

> **砂金：**我从不做赔本买卖的，希望各位「朋友」…别让我失望。

**Aventurine:** Please, this way if you will!

> **砂金：**来吧——这边请！

### Follow Aventurine

> *The "truth that could overturn Penacony" is just ahead... Good luck.*

**Aventurine:** Oh right, I remember mentioning something after that... What was it again?

> **砂金：**诶对了，好像那之后我还说了什么…是什么来着？

**Aventurine:** Ah... a familiar hallway, a familiar room. Do you remember? The last time we met was right here.

> **砂金：**啊…似曾相识的走廊，似曾相识的房间。还记得吗？我们上次见面，就是在这地方。

[Recap] The "truth" that Aventurine spoke of was behind this door. He told you to hold your breath and look forward to it. This moment resembled the time when you confronted Aventurine in the guest room. You opened the door and walked into the room.

*(Open door)*

**Aventurine:** This is it, just beyond this door. Take a deep breath and get ready.

> **砂金：**我们到了，就在这扇门后。屏住呼吸，拭目以待吧——

- ▸ (Enter the room.)

> ▸ （进入房间。）

**Aventurine:** Oh, I remember... my friend! After that, we played an enjoyable game.

> **砂金：**哦，我可算想起来了…朋友！那之后，咱们玩了场愉快的游戏。

**Aventurine:** Tell me, doesn't this feel eerily familiar?

> **砂金：**看，此时此刻，恰如彼时彼刻…不是么？

**Aventurine:** I remember everything now. This was what I told you at that time...

> **砂金：**我完全想起来啦，那个时候，我是这么对你说的——

[Recap] Aventurine led you to your room in the hotel in the Dreamscape, asking once again to make a deal with you. You saw Robin lying in the Dreampool with a huge wound, gradually dissolving into dream bubbles.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Aventurine:** Look, friend.

> **砂金：**看吧，朋友。

**Aventurine:** The game has already begun.

> **砂金：**游戏已经开始了。

**Aventurine:** Allow me to make you an offer — one you can't refuse.

> **砂金：**和我做笔交易吧，你无法拒绝。

**Aventurine:** No reason to choose otherwise...

> **砂金：**没有理由……

**Aventurine:** ...and no other choices.

> **砂金：**——也没有余地。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Sunday received a report that "Death" had taken Robin and an intruder away. Sparkle transformed into Robin's likeness and spoke to him, instigating him to take revenge for his sister. Sunday proclaimed that the time was not ripe.

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**???:** Brother, I'm back.

> **？？？：**哥哥，我回来了。

**Sunday:** ...Welcome back. How's your preparation for the performance coming?

> **星期日：**…欢迎回来。演出准备得如何了？

**???:** It's fine. Don't worry.

> **？？？：**还好哦，放心。

**Sunday:** "It's fine"?

> **星期日：**「还好」？

**Sunday:** Hmm, this is not good... You're the pride of The Family. Don't let those unnecessary emotions affect your perfect pitch.

> **星期日：**嗯，这可不好…你是家族的骄傲，别让那些多余的情绪影响你完美的音韵。

**???:** I... know.

> **？？？：**我…知道啦。

**???:** Brother, you seem to be in low spirit... What's happened? Is it because of the Watchmaker's guest list?

> **？？？：**哥哥，你看起来有些消沉…发生什么事了？是那些收到「钟表匠」邀请的宾客吗？

**Sunday:** Yes, I received the report that... "Death" had taken some of them. Perhaps someone was behind it.

> **星期日：**是啊，我收到了报告…「死亡」带走了他们中的一些人。或许是受人指使。

**Sunday:** Ah, I'm sorry. I forgot you just came back. You probably wouldn't know about it.

> **星期日：**啊，抱歉。我忘记你才刚回来，应该不知道这件事。

**Sunday:** Somehow, a nightmare called "Death" has descended upon Penacony, striking indiscriminately, bringing spiritual death to all equally...

> **星期日：**不知从何时开始，名为「死亡」的梦魇在匹诺康尼降临，它对人进行无差别袭击，将精神的死亡平等地带给了所有人……

**Sunday:** In the utopian dreamscape envisioned by The Family, such sorrowful incidents shouldn't arise. It profoundly undermines the equilibrium and serenity of the dreamscape... How detestable.

> **星期日：**但家族构建的美梦中，任何不幸都不应发生。它严重破坏了梦境的秩序与和平…多么可恨。

**???:** I can't believe this has happened. Was... someone killed again?

> **？？？：**竟然发生了这种事，那…是又有人遇害了吗？

**Sunday:** Yes. There were two. One was a stowaway, the other...

> **星期日：**嗯，共有两位。一位偷渡犯，以及……

**Sunday:** ...was you.

> **星期日：**…你。

**Sunday:** That's enough Fool. Your deeds have saddened me.

> **星期日：**可以了，愚者。你的作为令我心寒。

**Sparkle:** Pretty sharp, aren't you, Chicken Wing Boy?

> **花火：**你很敏锐嘛，鸡翅膀男孩。

**Sunday:** The Odes of Harmony talks about honesty — "The words of a fool begins with foolishness and end in treacherous arrogance"...

> **星期日：**《谐乐颂》诚不我欺——「愚者的言语起头是愚昧，末尾是奸恶的狂妄」……

**Sunday:** ...Please leave. THEIR dreamscape doesn't welcome you.

> **星期日：**…请回吧，祂的梦境不欢迎你。

**Sparkle:** Oh come on, lighten up! Why so serious with all the quotations and references?

> **花火：**哎呀，别板着脸嘛～还一本正经地引经据典，干嘛这么严肃？

**Sparkle:** I'm just curious — Now things have come to this, is The Family still unwilling to fight? I mean, your darling sister's already a goner, right?

> **花火：**我只是想问问——事到如今，家族还不打算出手吗？你那可怜的妹妹已经牺牲了哦？

**Sparkle:** Really? Don't tell me you're not craving a little vengeance.

> **花火：**你…难道就不想为她报仇吗？

**Sunday:** It's not yet time. When the fated day arrives, I shall mete out justice with unyielding righteousness.

> **星期日：**尚不是时候。等到了命定的日子，我必按正直施行审判。

**Sparkle:** Wow, you can endure that much? Truly, a heart of ice you've got there. Hey, maybe we could actually get along?

> **花火：**厉害啊，这你都能忍～真是个冷血的家伙。嘿，我们说不定很聊得来哦？

**Sparkle:** How about this? I'll stand in for your dear sister at events — surely you don't want the world to hear the Charmony Festival's been called off?

> **花火：**要不这样吧，我可以代你那位妹妹出席各种场合——你肯定也不想让人们知道…谐乐大典已经无法举办了吧？

**Sunday:** The Family has a plan, and do not dishonor my dear sister with your deceitful tongue again, Fool.

> **星期日：**家族自有安排。别再用你那诡诈的舌头玷污我亲爱的妹妹，愚者。

**Sparkle:** Alright, alright~ Just putting it out there... If you're ever in a pinch, remember I've got your back. I mean, who could resist a guy rocking spikes on his wings?

> **花火：**好吧好吧~我只是想说…如果你需要，我随时都可以帮你哦？谁能拒绝一位在鸡翅膀上打钉饰的男孩呢？

**Sunday:** There's no need. The malefactor has been exposed under THEIR radiant gaze and will soon fall by their own machinations.

> **星期日：**不必了，凶手已经在祂的光照下露了马脚。用不了多久，他便会因自己的计谋跌倒在地。

**Sunday:** Should the transgressor fail to turn away from this path, THEIR sword will be honed, THEIR bow strung, causing the malevolence the perpetrator spreads to befall them.

> **星期日：**若他不回头，那祂的刀必磨快，弓必上弦，使恶人施加的毒害临到自己头上。

**Sunday:** When that time comes, the heathen will realize they are but a mere mortal, doomed to descend into the netherworld.

> **星期日：**等到那时，那不敬爱神的外邦人便知道自己不过是凡人，要坠落到阴间去……

**Sunday:** ...And I will join THEIR vanguard to announce this good news to you personally, "Watchmaker."

> **星期日：**…而我将成为祂的尖兵，亲自为你报喜——「钟表匠」。

*(Mission completed)*
